Motivation: 现有神经网络优化中，流形约束通常被统一施加到所有权重矩阵上，但Transformer的不同模块可能天然偏好不同的权重空间几何结构。该工作旨在验证这一假设，为模块特定的几何感知优化提供依据。

Method: 以GPT-2预训练为实验平台，对Manifold Muon方法进行层级别消融研究。系统比较了Stiefel约束和DGram约束在注意力层与MLP层上的四种分配组合（全Stiefel、全DGram、注意力-Stiefel/MLP-DGram、注意力-DGram/MLP-Stiefel），在同一超参数设定下评估训练稳定性和性能。

Result: 注意力层用Stiefel、MLP层用DGram的配置性能最优；反向配置（注意力-DGram/MLP-Stiefel）和全DGram配置在共享超参数下变得不稳定。失败原因追踪为：DGram约束下注意力权重的奇异值持续增长，放大了注意力logits，进而导致softmax饱和。

Conclusion: Transformer的对称性感知和几何感知优化应是模块特定的，而非统一施加。注意力层与MLP层对权重流形几何存在明确的不对称偏好，这对设计更优的优化算法具有指导意义。

Abstract: Weight-space geometry plays a central role in neural network optimization, yet manifold constraints are often applied uniformly across all weight matrices. In this work, we ask whether different transformer modules prefer different manifold geometries. We study Manifold Muon for GPT-2 pretraining and compare layer-wise assignments of Stiefel and DGram constraints across attention and MLP blocks. Our results show a clear asymmetry: constraining attention layers with Stiefel geometry while assigning DGram geometry to MLP layers gives the best performance among the tested configurations, whereas the inverted assignment and all-DGram configuration become unstable under the shared hyperparameter setting. We trace this failure to singular value growth in DGram-constrained attention weights, which can amplify attention logits and induce softmax saturation. These findings suggest that symmetry-aware and geometry-aware optimization for transformers should be module-specific rather than uniform.

</details>


### [246] [Once-for-All: Scalable Simultaneous Forecasting via Equilibrium State Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13285)
*Beinan Xu,Andy Song,Jiti Gao,Feng Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出平衡状态估计（ESE）范式，通过先估计系统间平衡态再生成整体预测，实现多系统同步预测，在保持SOTA精度的同时获得10-70倍加速，且具备线性复杂度、鲁棒性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实场景（如经济、医疗建模）中常需对多个相互关联的系统进行同时预测，现有方法逐个系统预测，效率低下；需要一种能一次性完成所有系统预测且保持高精度的新范式。

Method: 提出ESE范式：首先估计跨系统的平衡状态（equilibrium state），然后基于当前状态与估计平衡态之间的差异生成整体预测（holistic forecasts），单次前向传播即可完成所有系统的预测。

Result: 在合成数据及真实数据集（货币汇率、COVID-19传播建模）上，ESE精度与SOTA方法相当甚至更优，速度显著更快；与常规预测器无缝集成后可实现10-70倍加速；线性时间复杂度使其随系统数量增加的扩展性远超SOTA；在多种扰动下保持准确性。

Conclusion: ESE是一种快速、可泛化、鲁棒且可扩展的多系统同步预测方法，兼具高精度与高效率，适合大规模多系统预测场景。

Abstract: We introduce Equilibrium State Estimation (ESE), a novel paradigm for simultaneous prediction, where multiple interacting systems require separate yet coordinated forecasts. Such scenarios often arise in real-world settings such as economics and healthcare modeling. Unlike existing approaches that predict one system at a time, ESE forecasts all systems in a single pass. It first estimates the equilibrium state across systems, then generates holistic forecasts based on the difference between the current state and the estimated equilibrium. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets, including currency exchange and COVID-19 spread modeling, demonstrate that ESE is at least as accurate as state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods while being significantly faster. In addition, ESE integrates seamlessly with conventional predictors, combining their accuracy with its exceptional efficiency and delivering a 10-70x speedup. With linear-time complexity, ESE scales far better than SOTA methods as the number of systems increases. Moreover, it remains accurate under diverse perturbations, establishing ESE as a fast, generalizable, robust, and scalable multi-prediction method.

</details>


### [247] [Clipping Makes Distributed and Federated Asynchronous SGD Robust to Stragglers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13287)
*Samuel Erickson,Mikael Johansson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为异步SGD中梯度裁剪的稳定化效果提供了理论证明，表明裁剪消除了oracle复杂度对最大延迟的依赖，并在sub-Weibull噪声模型下首次给出了异步优化的高概率收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步SGD虽能最大化硬件利用率，但恒定步长下慢worker导致的大延迟会损害收敛性。实践中发现梯度裁剪能"稳定"深度学习的异步训练，但缺乏理论解释，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 采用sub-Weibull分布对梯度噪声建模（推广sub-Gaussian和sub-exponential至更重尾分布），分析带梯度裁剪的异步SGD，证明裁剪后oracle复杂度与最大延迟解耦，并给出期望收敛和高概率收敛两种分析。

Result: 梯度裁剪消除了oracle复杂度对最大延迟的依赖；在sub-Weibull噪声假设下，证明了期望收敛，并首次在异步优化中给出高概率收敛结果。

Conclusion: 梯度裁剪是异步SGD应对慢worker和重尾噪声的有效机制，理论结果解释了实践中观察到的稳定化现象，为异步分布式训练提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: In modern machine learning, parallelization of training is an important strategy for increasing scale. Asynchronous stochastic gradient descent (ASGD), which maximizes the utilization of available hardware by avoiding waiting for slow workers. However, with constant step sizes, the convergence of ASGD is nonetheless affected negatively by slow workers due to large delays in updates. At the same time, it has been empirically observed in asynchronous training of deep learning models that gradient clipping "stabilizes" training. In this work, we provide a theoretical justification for this behavior, as we show that clipping removes the dependence of the maximum delay in the oracle complexity. We employ a sub-Weibull model of gradient noise which generalizes sub-Gaussian and sub-exponential distributions to more heavy-tailed distributions, motivated by empirical observations in deep learning. We show convergence in expectation, and the first time in asynchronous optimization, convergence with high probability.

</details>


### [248] [Quantizing Time-Series Models As Dynamical Systems: Trajectory-Based Quantization Sensitivity Score](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13300)
*Mariya Pavlova,Harrison Bo Hua Zhu,Elizsveta Semenova,Yingzhen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于轨迹的量化敏感度评分（TQS），将训练后量化重新构建为离散时间动力系统的稳定性问题，量化误差在 rollout 过程中的传播与放大被表征，从而实现与量化器解耦的先验敏感度估计，并基于此构建无需校准数据的混合精度量化框架 TQS-PTQ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 PTQ 方法中敏感度分析与量化过程紧密耦合，难以在量化前进行预算规划，尤其对于黑盒或算子融合后的编译网络。需要一种先验的、与量化器选择及位宽分配解耦的敏感度估计方法。

Method: 将网络推理过程建模为离散时间动力系统，定义 TQS 指标来刻画量化误差沿推理轨迹的传播与放大程度。基于 TQS，提出 TQS-PTQ 混合精度框架，实现无校准数据、无二阶近似的量化位宽分配。

Result: 实验表明，基于动力系统视角的 TQS-PTQ 在资源受限场景下实现了鲁棒且高性能的低精度部署。

Conclusion: 动力系统视角为低精度部署提供了一条鲁棒且高性能的路径，TQS 实现了敏感度分析与量化过程的解耦，具有广泛的适用性。

Abstract: We introduce the Trajectory-based Quantization Sensitivity Score (TQS), a metric that reframes post-training quantization (PTQ) through the lens of dynamical-systems stability. By modeling the network's rollout as a discrete-time dynamical system, TQS characterizes how quantization-induced errors propagate and amplify over the rollout horizon. Unlike conventional PTQ methods, where sensitivity analysis is often coupled to the quantization procedure, TQS enables a priori sensitivity estimation decoupled from quantizer selection and bit-width assignment. This separation allows for quantization budget planning even for black-box or compiled networks with fused operators. Building on this, we present TQS-PTQ, a flexible mixed-precision framework that requires no calibration data or costly second-order approximations. Our experiments show that a dynamical-systems perspective provides a robust, high-performing pathway for low-precision deployment in resource-constrained settings.

</details>


### [249] [Rarity-Gated Context Conditioning for Offline Imitation Learning-Based Maritime Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13311)
*Yongmin Kim,ByeongHoon Jeon,Sungil Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RGFiLM模块，通过数据驱动的稀有度门控机制调节上下文条件化的强度，解决上下文异常检测中因上下文分布极度不均衡导致罕见场景下误报率高的问题，在海上轨迹异常检测任务上取得最优F1-FPR权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际部署中上下文分布常存在严重的长尾/不均衡问题，罕见上下文场景往往是关键信息，但上下文条件化模型在罕见上下文中会产生不稳定决策和大量误报，现有方法未显式考虑上下文稀有度这一因素。

Method: 提出RGFiLM（Rarity-Gated Feature-wise Linear Modulation），将特征级线性调制（FiLM：对隐层特征进行上下文条件化的缩放和平移）与一个由数据驱动的稀有度评分控制的门控机制相结合。稀有度评分从上下文变量的经验分布中估计，门控在罕见上下文下更"果断"地施加调制，在常见上下文下则保持保守。

Result: 在基于AIS运动序列与ERA5环境上下文的敏感绕航场景海上轨迹异常检测任务上，RGFiLM在序列异常评分流程中实现了所有对比方法（含上下文无关和上下文条件化方法）中最优的平均F1-FPR权衡。

Conclusion: 显式建模上下文稀有度是降低上下文敏感异常检测中误报率的有效方法，RGFiLM为不均衡上下文分布下的异常检测提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Contextual anomaly detection aims to identify abnormal behavior conditional on context variables, but practical deployments often face highly imbalanced context distributions where rare regimes can be critical information. Under such frequency bias, context-conditioned models can produce unstable decisions and excessive false alarms in rare contexts. We propose Rarity-Gated Feature-wise Linear Modulation (RGFiLM), a rarity-aware conditioning module that combines feature-wise modulation (i.e., context-conditioned scaling and shifting of hidden features) with a gate controlled by a data-driven rarity score. The rarity score is estimated from the empirical distribution of context variables and regulates how strongly context modulates intermediate representations: the gate becomes more decisive under rare contexts while remaining conservative under frequent contexts. We evaluate RGFiLM on maritime trajectory anomaly detection using AIS motion sequences with ERA5 environmental context in an environment-sensitive detour scenario. When instantiated in a sequential anomaly scoring pipeline, RGFiLM achieves the best mean F1--False Positive Rate (FPR) trade-off among the compared context-agnostic and context-conditioned methods. These results suggest that explicitly accounting for context rarity is an effective approach for reducing false alarms in context-sensitive anomaly detection.

</details>


### [250] [Navigating the Safety-Fidelity Trade-off: Massive-Variate Time Series Forecasting for Power Systems via Probabilistic Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13338)
*Kaijie Xu,Anqi Wang,Xilin Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PowerPhase是一个大规模概率预测基准，涵盖6个输电网络（最高36,964个联合预测通道），引入安全约束感知评估指标；PowerForge是一种场景分位数预测器，通过类型特定解码头和因果桥接机制，在所有电网中取得最佳平均排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多变量概率预测基准存在两个关键缺陷：公共基准最多仅2,000个通道，电力系统基准则缺乏时序结构或概率评估。实际电力系统中，不同通道（如发电机、负荷、线路）具有差异化的物理特性和运行约束，现有基准无法在大规模条件下同时评估分布精度与约束满足能力。

Method: (1) 提出PowerPhase基准，基于6个输电网络的AC潮流求解生成目标轨迹，规模从2,000到36,964个通道。引入Safety_mBrier（安全修改Brier评分）、NECV（归一化期望约束违反）、CVaR-alpha等约束感知指标，与CRPS和Distortion互补。(2) 提出PowerForge预测器：采用场景基分位数预测架构，设计类型特定解码头以处理异构通道物理特性，并在变量组之间引入因果桥接模块以建模跨类型依赖关系。

Result: 在8个基线模型和3个随机种子的评估中，分布精度指标与约束满足指标对模型排名存在显著差异，作者将这种权衡命名为"safety-fidelity"（安全-保真度权衡）。PowerForge在所有6个电网测试中均取得最佳平均排名。

Conclusion: 大规模多变量概率预测需要同时关注分布精度和物理约束满足，两者不可偏废。PowerPhase提供了首个将两者统一评估的大规模基准，PowerForge证明了通过架构设计（类型特定解码+因果桥接）可以有效缓解safety-fidelity权衡。

Abstract: Probabilistic forecasting models are increasingly deployed on multivariate systems with distinct channel physics and operational constraints, but existing benchmarks evaluate neither property at scale. Public canonical multivariate benchmarks cap out at 2,000 channels, while power-system benchmarks either lack temporal structure or probabilistic evaluation. We introduce PowerPhase, a probabilistic forecasting benchmark built on six transmission grids ranging from 2,000 to 36,964 jointly forecasted channels, more than an order of magnitude beyond popular canonical multivariate benchmarks. Each target trajectory is the output of an AC power-flow solve, and PowerPhase ships with constraint-aware metrics, including Safety_mBrier, NECV, and CVaR-alpha, that complement CRPS and Distortion. Across eight baselines and three seeds, distributional accuracy and constraint satisfaction rank models differently, a trade-off we term safety-fidelity. We further propose PowerForge, a scenario-based quantile forecaster with type-specific decoding heads and a causal bridge between variable groups, which achieves the best average rank on every grid.

</details>


### [251] [Enhanced Low-Density Region Exploration in Classifier-Guided Diffusion Models Through Modified Reverse Diffusion Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13347)
*Jagriti Singh,Shekhar Verma,Muneendra Ojha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种纯采样阶段的密度感知扩展方法，无需额外训练即可引导扩散模型生成尾部（低密度）样本，通过修改分类器梯度将轨迹引向低置信度区域，同时引入预测真实图像引导以保持数据流形保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准分类器引导将概率质量集中在类条件分布的高密度均值附近，导致尾部稀有样本覆盖不足；现有尾部采样方法需额外训练网络，而加速/蒸馏技术未解决长尾覆盖问题。

Method: 在预训练的条件扩散模型和分类器基础上，修改引导反向动力学：(1) 通过修改后的分类器梯度将采样轨迹引向低置信度区域，探索低概率样本；(2) 在每个时间步引导采样过程朝向预测的真实图像，确保生成样本接近真实数据流形。两重引导均在噪声图像层面而非预测噪声层面施加。

Result: 在ImageNet上，所提采样器在64×64分辨率下持续提升ADM模型召回率，同时保持可比的FID；在256×256分辨率下展示了两重引导不同组合的视觉效果，并表明标准ADM分类器引导与预测真实图像引导结合可生成高感知质量样本。

Conclusion: 该方法在不增加额外训练的前提下，通过采样阶段的密度感知双重引导，有效改善了扩散模型对长尾分布中稀有样本的覆盖，同时保持了生成质量。

Abstract: Diffusion models have emerged as state-of-the-art generative models for high-fidelity image synthesis, particularly in their classifier-free guided and classifier-guided forms. However, standard classifier guidance concentrates probability mass around high-density class mean, leading to poor coverage of rare samples in the tails of the class-conditional distributions. Recent work on diffusion-based tail sampling mitigates this by training an additional low-density-seeking classifier with a synthetic-vs-real discriminator, at the cost of additional networks and training. In parallel, a number of samplers and distillation techniques accelerate or refine diffusion sampling, but do not explicitly address long-tail coverage. We propose a purely sampling-time, density-aware extension of classifier-guided conditional diffusion model that targets low-density regions without any additional training. We have applied guidance at noisy images not on predicted noise like most diffusion models. Starting from a pretrained conditional diffusion model and classifier on ImageNet, we modify the guided reverse dynamics by steering trajectories toward low-confidence regions via the modified classifier gradient, and at each time step, we also guide the sampling process toward the predicted real image. 1st guidance helps explore low-probability samples, and 2nd guidance helps to generate samples to be close to the real data manifold. The proposed sampler consistently improves ADM model recall at 64x64 resolution while maintaining a comparable FID, and with a 256x256 ADM model, we showed the results visually with different combinations of both guidance. We also showed that standard ADM classifier guidance, combined with predicted real image guidance, helps generate high perceptual quality samples with a 256x256 ADM model on ImageNet.

</details>


### [252] [Positional Encoding in the Context of Memristor-Based Analog Computation for Automatic Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13379)
*Benedikt Hilmes,Nick Rossenbach,Ralf Schlüter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了忆阻器上执行Transformer位置编码时，因大输出值导致模数转换(ADC)精度严重下降的问题。作者提出通过调整特定层的ADC权重与精度位宽配比，可将执行退化降低约50%，且不增加能耗；在无法修改ADC的情况下，移除编码相关的线性变换也能减少约30%的退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 忆阻器虽能以模拟方式高效执行向量-矩阵乘法，从而为NLP神经模型提供资源高效的推理方案，但当前的忆阻器计算在权重编程和执行过程中存在较大失真。作者特别识别出，变换后的位置编码产生的大输出值会在模数转换(ADC)环节造成严重性能退化，这一问题此前未被充分关注和解决。

Method: (1) 定位问题：识别出Transformer中位置编码经线性变换后产生的较大输出值，是导致忆阻器ADC环节退化的关键原因。(2) 方案一（ADC可调）：针对特定忆阻器层，调整ADC的权重位宽与精度位宽的配比，在保持能耗稳定的前提下降低执行退化。(3) 方案二（ADC不可调）：移除与位置编码相关的线性变换，从源头上削减大输出值，从而缓解ADC退化。

Result: 方案一（调整ADC位宽配比）使执行退化相对降低约50%，同时估计能耗保持稳定；方案二（移除编码相关线性变换）使退化相对降低约30%。两种方案均显著改善了忆阻器上Transformer模型的推理质量。

Conclusion: 通过针对性的ADC位宽配置优化或结构简化，可有效缓解忆阻器计算中因位置编码大值输出引发的ADC精度瓶颈，为忆阻器在NLP推理中的实用化提供了可行的缓解路径。

Abstract: Memristors provide a new chance for resource-efficient computation of neural models for natural language processing by enabling analog execution of vector-matrix-multiplication. Yet, computations on these devices are currently subject to larger distortion, both in weight programming and execution. In this work, we identify large output values of transformed positional encodings to cause major degradation within analog-to-digital conversion (ADC) as part of memristor-based computation. By adjusting the proportion of weight and precision bits of the ADC of specific memristor layers, we reduce the degradation of the execution by ~50% relative, while keeping the estimated energy consumption stable. Additionally, we investigate scenarios where the ADC cannot be modified. In that case the degradation can be reduced by ~30% relative after removing encoding-related linear transformations.

</details>


### [253] [Hölder++: Improving the Quality-Coherence Trade-off in Multimodal VAEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13381)
*Huyen Vo,María Martínez-García,Isabel Valera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Hölder++ 是一种新型多模态 VAE，通过无近似的 Hölder 池化、共享与私有表示的分离架构以及层次化推断，显著改善了生成质量与跨模态一致性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态 VAE 在生成质量（真实性与多样性）和跨模态语义一致性之间存在难以调和的权衡。最近的 MMVAE+ 虽用 Hölder 池化近似改善了 coherence，但牺牲了多样性。本文旨在突破这一瓶颈。

Method: 提出 Hölder++：(i) 首次在多模态 VAE 中实现无近似的精确 Hölder 池化；(ii) 扩展架构 (Hölder+) 分别建模共享表示与模态特定的私有表示；(iii) 引入层次化推断 (Hölder++)，进一步增强共享与私有表示的 disentanglement。

Result: 实验证实 Hölder++ 持续改善了生成质量与 coherence 的权衡，产生了更结构化的潜在空间，且学习到的共享表示对下游任务具有更强的信息性。

Conclusion: Hölder++ 通过精确池化、公私分离及层次化推断三方面创新，有效缓解了多模态生成中长期存在的质量-一致性困境，并为下游任务提供了更优的共享表示。

Abstract: Existing approaches for multimodal variational autoencoders (VAEs) face a trade-off between generative quality and coherence-i.e., they struggle to generate realistic and diverse samples that, at the same time, are semantically consistent across modalities. A recent work shows that using a simple approximation to Hölder pooling as an aggregation method improves coherence over the SOTA MMVAE+, despite assuming a single shared representation across all modalities. Yet, it slightly compromises sample diversity. Inspired by this insight, we propose Hölder++, a novel multimodal VAE that improves the generative quality-coherence trade-off through: (i) the first implementation of Hölder pooling without any approximation for multimodal VAEs; (ii) an extended architecture that models distinct shared and private (i.e., modality-specific) representations (Hölder+); and (iii) hierarchical inference that further enhances the disentanglement between the shared and private representations (Hölder++). Our experiments corroborate that Hölder++ consistently improves the generative quality-coherence trade-off, yields more structured latent spaces, and learns shared representations that are informative for downstream tasks.

</details>


### [254] [PolyFlow: Safe and Efficient Polytope-Constrained Flow Matching with Constraint Embedding and Projection-free Update](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13400)
*Jianming Ma,Qiyue Yang,Yang Zhang,Liyun Yan,Zhanxiang Cao,Yazhou Zhang,Yue Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PolyFlow 是一种多面体约束流匹配框架，通过离散时间流公式和无投影架构，将任意多面体约束直接嵌入生成模型，实现零约束违反，同时保持高分布保真度并显著降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于流的生成模型在安全关键物理系统中部署时面临严格的约束要求。现有方法通过事后修正来满足安全性，但这会带来高昂的计算开销，并可能扭曲学习到的分布。因此需要一种能够将约束直接内嵌于模型和流动力学中的方法。

Method: 提出 PolyFlow 框架，核心包含两部分：(1) 离散时间流公式，消除离散化误差；(2) 无投影架构，无需昂贵的迭代求解器即可保证任意多面体（polyhedral）约束的严格满足。约束被直接嵌入模型与流动力学之中。

Result: 在多项规划与控制任务中，PolyFlow 实现了零约束违反，同时保持高分布保真度。与最先进的约束生成基线相比，显著降低了推理延迟，并在安全性、效率和生成质量之间展现了良好的权衡。

Conclusion: PolyFlow 证明了将多面体约束直接嵌入流匹配框架是可行且高效的，为安全关键物理系统中的生成模型部署提供了一种有效方案，兼顾了安全性、效率与生成质量。

Abstract: While flow-based generative models have demonstrated strong performance across a wide range of domains, deploying them in safety-critical physical systems remains challenging due to strict constraint requirements. Existing approaches typically enforce safety through post-hoc corrections, which incur substantial computational overhead and may distort the learned distribution. We propose PolyFlow, a polytope-constrained flow matching framework that embeds constraints directly into the model and flow dynamics. PolyFlow introduces a discrete-time flow formulation and a projection-free architecture, which eliminate the discretization error and guarantee strict satisfaction of arbitrary polyhedral constraints, without the need for expensive iterative solvers. Experimental results show that PolyFlow achieves zero constraint violation while maintaining high distributional fidelity across a range of planning and control tasks. Compared to state-of-the-art constrained generation baselines, PolyFlow significantly reduces inference latency and demonstrates a favorable trade-off between safety, efficiency, and generative quality. Code is available on https://github.com/MJianM/PolyFlow.

</details>


### [255] [How Much Memory Do We Need? Adaptive Memory Gate for Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13443)
*Jihyeon Hur,Yongseok Kwon,Min-Gi Jo,Jeongwhan Choi,Noseong Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AMGFNO提出了一种自适应记忆门控的傅里叶神经算子，通过可学习的门控机制动态调制记忆权重，解决了固定记忆权重在不同观测条件（分辨率、物理参数）下适应性不足的问题，在低分辨率下实现了55-79%的nRMSE降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强神经算子在处理时变偏微分方程时，对所有观测条件统一施加固定记忆权重，但初步实验表明最优记忆权重随分辨率和物理参数（如粘性）变化而显著不同，固定权重无法同时优化不同设置下的性能。

Method: 提出AMGFNO（Adaptive Memory Gated Fourier Neural Operator），通过引入可学习的门控机制，使记忆权重能够根据输入条件动态调节。该门控模块嵌入在傅里叶神经算子框架中，实现对历史状态信息的自适应融合。

Result: 在Kuramoto-Sivashinsky方程和Burgers方程上的实验表明，AMGFNO在低分辨率下相比基线方法实现了55-79%的标准化均方根误差（nRMSE）降低；同时观察到学习到的门控值随分辨率增加从约0.7自动衰减至接近零。

Conclusion: 自适应记忆门控机制有效解决了固定记忆权重的局限性，使得神经算子能够灵活适应不同的观测条件，显著提升了低分辨率场景下的长期预测精度，且门控行为与直觉一致——高分辨率时更多依赖当前观测、低分辨率时更多依赖历史记忆。

Abstract: Neural operators have emerged as a powerful data-driven approach for solving time-dependent PDEs. Among recent advances, memory-augmented neural operators explicitly incorporate past states and have achieved remarkable performance under low-resolution observation settings. However, existing approaches apply a fixed memory weight regardless of observation conditions, such as resolution or physical parameters, limiting their adaptability. Our preliminary experiments reveal that optimal memory weight varies with resolution and viscosity, implying that a fixed memory weight cannot simultaneously optimize performance across diverse settings. We propose AMGFNO, which dynamically modulates memory weight through a learnable gate. On the Kuramoto-Sivashinsky and Burgers' equations, AMGFNO achieves 55-79% nRMSE reduction over at low resolution, with the learned gate value automatically decreasing from $\bar{g} \approx 0.7$ to near-zero as resolution increases.

</details>


### [256] [Clustering Node Attributed Networks with Graph Neural Networks and Self Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13444)
*Rodrigo de Sapienza Luna,Daniel Ratton Figueiredo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于图神经网络的自监督图聚类框架，通过多轮自学习迭代优化节点表示和聚类结果，在合成和真实数据集上展现出竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图聚类方法仅利用网络结构或节点属性中的单一信息源。现实数据中，节点往往同时具有网络连接和属性信息，且两者可能都存在噪声或不完整性。现有方法未能充分在无监督条件下同时且迭代地挖掘这两种信息，因此需要一种能够自学习地融合边信息和节点属性的聚类框架。

Method: 提出一种多轮自学习的无监督图聚类框架。每轮中，GNN 基于当前图结构生成节点表示并用于聚类；本轮聚类结果反过来影响下一轮的图结构。同时，每轮基于原始图构建一个"上下文图"(context graph)，用于辅助节点表示学习。整个过程无需标签，通过多轮迭代逐步优化表示与聚类质量。

Result: 合成数据上，当网络或属性单独都不够信息丰富时，该方法能同时提取两者信息，优于仅利用单一信息源的算法；多轮学习持续提升性能，且始终优于等同时长的单轮训练。真实数据集上，在簇大小均衡时，方法性能与当前最先进方法相当。

Conclusion: 该框架有效融合图结构与节点属性信息，多轮自学习机制显著优于传统单轮GNN聚类方法，在信息不充分的场景下优势明显，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Graph clustering - partitioning the node set of a graph into disjoint subsets that reflect some latent information - is a fundamental problem as it finds applications in a myriad of different scenarios. While this classic problem has been tackled for decades by different communities, a recent variation of the problem driven by real data considers the scenario where nodes have attributes that are also informative. This has triggered novel methods that simultaneously leverage network information (edges) and node information (attributed) in the design of novel clustering algorithms. This work proposes a novel framework that builds on prior works that have applied graph neural networks (GNN) to graph clustering. The proposed framework operates in rounds of self learning in a fully unsupervised setting. In each round, a GNN generates representations for nodes that are used to cluster the nodes. This clustering influences the graph used to generate the node representation in the next round. Moreover, a context graph built in each round using the original graph is used to generate the node representations. Empirical results show that the proposed methodology extracts information from both network edges and node attributes in synthetic data, outperforming algorithms focused solely on the network or attributes when neither are very informative. Multiple rounds of learning also improve the performance and always outperforms a long single round of training (i.e., classic GNN graph clustering). When considering real datasets, empirical results indicate that the proposed methodology is competitive to state-of-the-art methods when cluster sizes are balanced.

</details>


### [257] [Uncertainty Estimation for Molecular Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13451)
*Paul Seij,Christian A. Naesseth,Stephan Mandt,Metod Jazbec*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于拉普拉斯近似的后验方法，用于估计预训练分子扩散模型中每个生成样本的不确定性，该不确定性分数与样本质量负相关，可用于过滤低质量样本以提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在3D分子生成中广泛应用，但缺乏原则性的信号来指示生成分子何时可能质量较低，需要一种可靠的不确定性量化方法。

Method: 基于去噪网络的拉普拉斯近似，在整个生成轨迹上测量噪声预测的变异性，作为每个样本的不确定性估计。

Result: 所得不确定性分数与已建立的样本级质量指标呈负相关，可用于过滤生成样本，通过测试时扩展提升模型性能。

Conclusion: 该方法有效且实用，首次为分子扩散模型提供了原则性的后验不确定性量化手段。

Abstract: Diffusion models have seen wide adoption for 3D molecular generation, yet they offer no principled signal of when a generated molecule is likely to be of low quality. We propose a post-hoc method for estimating per-sample uncertainty in pretrained molecular diffusion models. Building on a Laplace approximation of the denoising network, we measure the variability of the noise prediction across the generation trajectory. Empirically, we show that the resulting uncertainty score is informative of sample quality, exhibiting a negative correlation with established sample-level quality metrics. We further study how the proposed uncertainty score can be used to filter generated samples, improving model performance via test-time scaling.

</details>


### [258] [CRAFTIIF: Cross-Resolution Analytic Four-Type Interpretable Isolation Forest for Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13486)
*William Smits*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CRAFTIIF是一种统一的无监督异常检测框架，通过500个跨四种小波族的随机解析小波特征和五组结构化孤立森林，能同时检测点、分布、时间、集体四种异常类型，并内置可解释性。在mTSBench基准的19个数据集上，VUS-PR指标比此前最佳方法提升40.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元时间序列异常检测面临四种结构上截然不同的异常类型（点异常、分布异常、时间异常、集体异常），每种需要不同的特征表示，而现有无监督方法大多只针对一两种类型，且可解释性有限。因此需要一个统一的、能够覆盖全部四种类型且无需数据集特定调参的框架。

Method: 提出CRAFTIIF框架：(1) 从Morlet、DOG、Haar、Coiflet四个小波族中随机生成K=500个解析小波特征，每种小波族分别针对一种异常类型；(2) 构建五个结构化孤立森林——四个分别针对每种异常类型，一个元IF用于检测复合异常；(3) 采用自适应Otsu/MAD阈值根据异常率(0.1%~69.2%)自动校准检测；(4) 由于每个IF仅在类型特定特征上训练，分支激活天然提供异常类型归因，无需事后解释。

Result: 在mTSBench全部19个数据集上评估：平均F1=0.228（全部19个），F1=0.322（13个可检测数据集），在VUS-PR指标上排名25种评估方法第一（0.463 vs 此前最佳0.329，提升40.7%）。诊断框架（oracle F1、可检测性极限、分支分离比）识别出19个数据集中有6个对任何无监督方法都基本不可检测。11种消融条件证实自适应阈值（+38% F1）、四分支结构（+20%）、元IF（+23%）各自至关重要。

Conclusion: CRAFTIIF首次在一个统一的无监督框架中同时有效处理四种不同结构的异常类型，通过类型特定特征与结构化IF的协同设计实现了内在可解释性，在mTSBench基准上显著超越现有方法。诊断框架揭示部分数据集的根本不可检测性，为领域的评估标准提供了重要启示。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in multivariate time series is challenged by four structurally distinct anomaly types -- point (isolated spikes), distributional (level shifts), temporal (rhythm changes), and collective (inter-sensor correlation breakdowns) -- each requiring different feature representations. Most unsupervised methods target only one or two types and provide limited interpretability. We present CRAFTIIF (Cross-Resolution Analytic Four-Type Interpretable Isolation Forest), a fully unsupervised framework targeting all four types without dataset-specific tuning. CRAFTIIF generates K=500 random analytic wavelet feature draws across four families (Morlet, DOG, Haar, Coiflet), each targeting a specific anomaly type, feeding five structured Isolation Forests -- one per type plus a meta-IF for compound anomalies. An adaptive Otsu/MAD threshold calibrates detection automatically across anomaly rates from 0.1% to 69.2%. Because each IF is trained exclusively on type-specific features, branch firing provides direct anomaly-type attribution by construction, without post-hoc explanation. Evaluated on all 19 datasets of the mTSBench benchmark (Zhou et al., TMLR 2026), CRAFTIIF achieves mean F1=0.228 (all 19 datasets) and F1=0.322 (13 detectable datasets), ranking first among all 25 evaluated methods on VUS-PR (0.463 vs. previous best 0.329, +40.7%). A diagnostic framework -- oracle F1, detectability limits, and branch separation ratios -- identifies 6 of 19 datasets as fundamentally undetectable by any unsupervised method. Ablation over 11 conditions confirms adaptive thresholding (+38% F1), four-branch structure (+20%), and meta-IF (+23%) are each essential. Code: https://github.com/smitswil/craftiif

</details>


### [259] [A2D2: Fine-Tuning Any-Length Discrete Diffusion for Adaptive Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13565)
*Sophia Tang,Yuchen Zhu,Molei Tao,Pranam Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: A2D2 是一个统一的奖励引导微调框架，针对任意长度的离散扩散模型，通过联合优化插入和去掩码策略以及基于质量的推理调度，实现了理论上收敛到奖励倾斜分布，并在生成灵活性和准确性上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散扩散模型虽已扩展到任意长度生成，但缺乏针对任意长度设置的、有原则的奖励引导微调方法，现有工作多局限于固定长度或仅依赖推理时引导。

Method: 作者提出 A2D2 框架：(1) 推导联合插入-去掩码路径测度的 Radon-Nikodym 导数，理论上保证无需目标样本即可收敛到奖励倾斜分布；(2) 建立去掩码和插入质量作为解码误差最小化的可行度量；(3) 提出 Adaptive Joint Decoding (AJD) 损失，可证明地产生生成奖励倾斜分布的最优路径测度。

Result: 实验表明，A2D2 在奖励优化方面优于先前的固定长度微调和推理时引导方法，同时提升了生成的灵活性和准确性。

Conclusion: A2D2 首次为任意长度离散扩散模型提供了有理论保证的奖励引导微调框架，通过联合优化插入/去掩码策略和自适应推理调度，有效平衡了奖励优化与生成质量。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models offer a simple and stable likelihood-based framework for sequence generation, recently extended to any-length settings via token insertion. Principled reward-guided fine-tuning for any-length discrete diffusion, however, remains largely unexplored. We introduce Fine-Tuning Any-Length Discrete Diffusion for Adaptive Decoding (A2D2), a unified framework for reward-guided fine-tuning of any-length discrete diffusion models via joint optimization of the insertion and unmasking policies together with a quality-based inference schedule. We derive the Radon-Nikodym derivative for the joint insertion-unmasking path measures, enabling theoretically guaranteed convergence to the intractable reward-tilted sequence distribution without requiring target samples. Building on this, we establish unmasking and insertion quality as tractable approaches for minimizing decoding error and introduce the Adaptive Joint Decoding (AJD) loss, which provably yields the optimal path measure that generates the reward-tilted distribution. Empirically, A2D2 improves reward optimization while enhancing generation flexibility and accuracy over prior fixed-length fine-tuning and inference-time guidance methods.

</details>


### [260] [Adjusted Cup-Product Neural Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13568)
*Snigdha Chandan Khilar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文引入了一种称为"调整杯积神经层"的神经原语，将杯积与来自高规范理论的调整项硬连接，构造出设计上即具有规范不变性的读出层。主要理论结果表明，在闭合循环上，输出完全依赖于调整系数，调整项是规范不变信号的唯一来源，该可观测量为非零二次型，且在1-规范和2-规范变换下严格不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理与几何中许多重要可观测量是cochains的杯积，但如何在神经网络中构造一个内禀规范不变的杯积读出层是一个待解决的问题。作者旨在设计一种硬编码规范不变性的神经原语，使得网络输出不依赖于规范选择。

Method: 提出"调整杯积神经层"（adjusted cup product neural layer），将代数拓扑中的杯积运算与高规范理论（higher gauge theory）中的调整项相结合，作为神经网络的一个读出层。通过理论分析，研究该层在闭合循环上的行为，并证明其规范变换性质。

Result: 主要定理：在闭合循环上，网络输出完全由调整系数决定；若将该系数设为零，则无论其他参数如何，输出完全消失。因此调整项是规范不变信号的唯一来源。进一步证明该可观测量为非零二次型，且在1-规范和2-规范变换下具有严格不变性。

Conclusion: 调整杯积神经层提供了一种设计上即保证规范不变性的神经网络构件，调整项是产生规范不变信号的必要且唯一的机制，该结构在高规范理论背景下具有严格的数学保证。

Abstract: Many important observables in physics and geometry are cup products of cochains. The adjusted cup product neural layer has been introduced in this paper. It is a neural primitive that hard wires the cup product with an adjustment term from higher gauge theory. This creates a readout that is gauge invariant by design. Their main theoretical result shows that on a closed cycle the output relies entirely on the adjustment coefficient. Setting this coefficient to zero removes the output completely regardless of other parameters. Thus the adjustment is the only source of gauge invariant signal. They prove this observable is a nonzero quadratic form and is exactly invariant under one and two gauge transformations.

</details>


### [261] [Existence Precedes Value: Joint Modeling of Observational Existence and Evolving States in Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13571)
*Yifan Hu,Hongzhou Chen,Peiyuan Liu,Yiding Liu,Zewei Dong,Jiang-Ming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Timeflies是一个统一框架，将时间序列预测重新定义为未来可观测性推断与数值估计的联合问题，通过观测流和数值流两条耦合通道，显式建模观测动态与状态演化的交互，并提出Shadow基准和OVJE指标进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（如Neural ODEs、连续时间图网络）虽改进了对历史不规则性的建模，但在推理时依赖"先知假设"——即未来有效观测的时间戳已知。现实中，更根本的问题不仅是预测未来值，还包括是否存在有效观测。这种未来可观测性的不确定性在现有方法中被忽视。

Method: 提出Timeflies框架，包含观测流和数值流两条通道，通过三个专用模块耦合：(1) 可靠性感知嵌入模块；(2) 观测引导的依赖建模模块；(3) 联合预测模块。同时构建了Shadow基准数据集，融合公开数据集的自然缺失模式与真实工业数据，并引入观测-数值联合熵（OVJE）指标全面评估耦合预测性能。

Result: 大量实验表明，Timeflies在预测性能上一致优于现有方法，验证了在含缺失值的时间序列预测中显式建模未来可观测性的重要性。

Conclusion: 未来可观测性的显式建模是解决现实世界不规则时间序列预测问题的关键，Timeflies框架和Shadow基准为这一方向提供了有效的方法论和评估标准。

Abstract: Real-world time series are often highly incomplete and irregular due to sensor dormancy, transmission delays, and event-driven sampling, making reliable forecasting fundamentally challenging. Existing methods have evolved from impute-then-forecast pipelines to continuous-time models such as Neural ODEs and continuous-time graph networks. While these approaches improve the modeling of historical irregularity, they still rely on an implicit oracle assumption at inference time: the timestamps of future valid observations are presumed to be known in advance. This assumption limits practical relevance, since in many real systems the more fundamental question is not only what the future value will be, but also whether a valid observation will occur at all. In this paper, we propose Timeflies, a unified framework that reformulates forecasting as a joint problem of future observability inference and value estimation. To explicitly model the interaction between observation dynamics and state evolution, Timeflies adopts an observation stream and a value stream, coupled through three dedicated modules for reliability-aware embedding, observation-guided dependency modeling, and joint prediction. We further construct Shadow, a benchmark that combines natural missingness from public datasets with real-world industrial data, and introduce the Observation-Value Joint Entropy (OVJE) metric to comprehensively evaluate this coupled predictability. Extensive experiments show that Timeflies consistently outperforms existing methods, highlighting the importance of explicitly modeling future observability in time series forecasting with missing values. Code and dataset are available in https://github.com/ant-intl/Timeflies.

</details>


### [262] [Simplex-Constrained Sparse Bagging: Transitioning from Uniform Priors to Sparse Posteriors in Ensemble Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13589)
*Meher Sai Preetam,Meher Bhaskar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SCSB框架，通过在概率单纯形上联合优化集成剪枝与概率校准，引入凹二次惩罚项解决L1-单纯形悖论，实现高达96%的压缩率，同时提升校准性能并保持泛化精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准装袋集成方法对所有基学习器赋予均匀投票权，忽略了各学习器的局部能力差异，导致模型过度自信。现有L1正则化在单纯形上恒为常数，无法有效剪枝。

Method: 将集成剪枝与校准形式化为概率单纯形上的联合优化问题，最小化OOB损失；针对"L1-单纯形悖论"引入凹二次惩罚项以诱导稀疏性。方法模型无关，适用于随机森林、装袋SVM和装袋神经网络等。

Result: 实现高达96%的集成压缩率，获得线性推理加速，在保持或提升泛化精度的同时显著降低预期校准误差（ECE）。

Conclusion: SCSB是一种数学严谨的后训练压缩与校准框架，有效解决了装袋集成的冗余和过自信问题，具有广泛的模型适用性和实际部署价值。

Abstract: We present Simplex-Constrained Sparse Bagging (SCSB), a mathematically rigorous framework for post-training compression and probability calibration of bootstrap-based bagging ensembles. Standard bagging ensembles (such as Random Forests, Bagged SVMs, and Bagged Neural Networks) assign uniform voting power to all constituent estimators. However, this naive uniform prior ignores the varying local competence of base estimators and contributes to model overconfidence. We formulate ensemble pruning and calibration as a joint optimization problem over the probability simplex by minimizing the Out-Of-Bag (OOB) loss. To induce sparsity, we address the theoretical "L1-simplex paradox" -- the mathematical reality that the L1 norm is constant on the simplex and fails to prune -- by introducing a concave quadratic penalty. SCSB is model-agnostic and achieves up to 96% ensemble compression, yielding linear inference speedups and superior probability calibration (lowered Expected Calibration Error) while preserving or enhancing generalization accuracy.

</details>


### [263] [The Stable Recovery Manifold: Geometric Principles Governing Recoverability in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13637)
*Ayushman Trivedi,Bhavika Melwani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文基于可及性崩塌（Accessibility Collapse）框架，探究了持续学习中灾难性遗忘的几何结构。通过在Split CIFAR-100上训练ResNet-18，发现遗忘知识的恢复子空间维度保持稳定（均值k_t=8.0），表明灾难性遗忘本质上是可及性与流形对齐问题，而非信息破坏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点将灾难性遗忘视为先前知识的破坏。作者旨在从几何视角重新审视这一现象，检验遗忘究竟是信息的真正丢失，还是表征重组导致的可及性问题，从而深化对持续学习中遗忘机制的理解。

Method: 采用Split CIFAR-100数据集和顺序训练的ResNet-18，跨十个任务分析可恢复性、表征漂移和恢复复杂度。提出恢复子空间维度（Recovery Subspace Dimensionality, k_t）作为新指标，衡量保留90%完整探针性能所需的最小奇异方向数；通过主角度漂移与可恢复性的相关性分析，以及构建几何模型解释可恢复性方差。

Result: 尽管存在显著的表征漂移，恢复维度在整个训练过程中保持稳定（均值k_t=8.0），反驳了"可恢复性扩散假说"。主角度漂移与可恢复性呈强相关（r=-0.862），简单几何模型解释了82.2%的可恢复性方差，支持"稳定恢复流形假说"。

Conclusion: 灾难性遗忘主要是可及性（accessibility）和流形对齐（manifold-alignment）问题，而非信息的不可逆破坏。遗忘的知识尽管经历了表征重组，仍以紧凑可解码的形式存在，这对持续学习算法的设计具有重要启示。

Abstract: Catastrophic forgetting is often viewed as the destruction of previously learned knowledge during sequential learning. Building on the Accessibility Collapse framework, we investigate the geometric structure of recoverability in continual learning. Using Split CIFAR-100 and a sequentially trained ResNet-18, we analyze recoverability, representational drift, and recovery complexity across ten tasks. We introduce Recovery Subspace Dimensionality (k_t), a measure of the minimum number of singular directions required to preserve 90 percent of full probe performance. Contrary to our Recoverability Diffusion hypothesis, recovery dimensionality remains stable throughout training (mean k_t = 8.0) despite substantial representational drift. Principal-angle drift strongly predicts recoverability (r = -0.862), and a simple geometric model explains 82.2 percent of recoverability variance. These findings support the Stable Recovery Manifold hypothesis, suggesting that forgotten knowledge remains compactly decodable despite representational reorganization. The results indicate that catastrophic forgetting is primarily an accessibility and manifold-alignment problem rather than information destruction.

</details>


### [264] [Dense Supervision, Sparse Updates: On the Sparsity and Geometry of On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13657)
*Guo Yu,Wenlin Liu,Yulan Hu,Hao-Xuan Ma,Jun-Peng Jiang,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了在策略蒸馏（OPD）对模型参数的影响，发现OPD更新具有坐标稀疏性（集中于FFN层）、数值满秩但谱集中、偏离权重主奇异子空间、且偏向于接近零的权重坐标。稀疏子网络可恢复大部分性能，但SGD不如AdamW，因密集监督保留了梯度尺度的异质性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPD结合了在策略学生轨迹和密集教师监督，已成为重要的后训练方法，但其对模型参数的具体影响机制尚不清楚。理解这一机制有助于解释OPD的有效性来源，并指导更高效的训练策略设计。

Method: 在多个语言模型和视觉-语言模型对上，从稀疏性和几何两个维度分析OPD的参数更新特性。包括：逐坐标稀疏度分析、层级分布统计、子网络剪枝实验、优化器消融（SGD vs AdamW）、更新矩阵的谱分析（秩、奇异值分布）、与源权重的主子空间对齐程度、以及按源权重值分层的更新幅度分布。

Result: （1）稀疏性：OPD更新小且坐标稀疏，跨层分布但集中于FFN；仅训练发现的子网络可恢复接近完整OPD的性能；但SGD优化器不如AdamW，因密集监督保留了各坐标梯度的异质尺度，AdamW的自适应缩放仍然有效。（2）几何：更新矩阵数值满秩但谱能量集中；更新方向主要偏离源权重的主奇异子空间；更新不成比例地落在源权重接近零的坐标上。

Conclusion: 密集教师监督并未将OPD变为普通的密集参数重写；相反，OPD保留了在策略后训练的重要几何特征。这一发现为理解和改进基于蒸馏的后训练方法提供了参数层面的见解。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (\textsc{OPD}) has recently become a prominent post-training recipe as it combines two desirable ingredients: on-policy student trajectories and dense teacher supervision, yet how this hybrid changes a model's parameters remains unclear. Across several language and vision-language model pairs and use cases, our analysis yields two main findings. On sparsity, \textsc{OPD}-style updates are small and coordinate-sparse. They are distributed across layers and are usually FFN-heavy. This sparse structure is operationally useful: training only the discovered subnetwork recovers nearly the same performance as full \textsc{OPD}. However, the sparsity-inducing SGD optimizer underperforms AdamW in our optimizer ablation, likely because dense teacher supervision preserves heterogeneous coordinate-wise gradient scales where AdamW's adaptive scaling remains useful. On geometry, the updates are numerically full-rank but spectrally concentrated; they lie mostly away from the principal singular subspaces of the source weights and fall disproportionately on coordinates where the source weights are close to zero. These findings suggest that dense teacher supervision does not turn \textsc{OPD} into ordinary dense parameter rewriting; instead, \textsc{OPD} retains important geometric signatures of on-policy post-training.

</details>


### [265] [Understanding Truncated Positional Encodings for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13671)
*James Flora,Mitchell Black,Weng-Keen Wong,Amir Nayyeri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究了截断位置编码（PE）在图神经网络中的表达能力差异。理论上证明截断后，谱方法和游走方法的表达能力不再等价，且截断的谱PE不再强于1-WL测试；实验表明混合使用多种截断PE优于单一家族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然完整版的谱PE和游走PE在表达能力上等价（介于1-WL和3-WL之间），但完整版需要O(n³)复杂度，实践者普遍使用截断版本（如前k个特征空间或邻接矩阵的前k次幂）。然而，截断PE的理论性质尚不明确，这驱动了本项研究。

Method: 从理论上对比分析截断条件下不同家族PE的表达能力差异：比较截断的谱PE（Laplacian特征空间、k-调和距离）与截断的游走PE（邻接矩阵多项式）在Weisfeiler-Lehman测试框架下的表现力，并给出截断谱PE不优于1-WL的推论。实验上在真实数据集上评估单一截断PE与混合截断PE的实际效果。

Result: 理论上证明截断后不同家族PE表达能力存在根本性差异；截断的谱PE不再强于1-WL测试；揭示了即使密切相关的截断谱PE（如k-调和距离）之间也存在表达能力差异；实验表明混合截断PE在实际数据集上优于任何单一家族。

Conclusion: 截断操作对不同家族PE的表达能力产生根本性影响，打破了完整版本下的等价性。实践建议是采用多种截断PE的混合策略以提升GNN性能。

Abstract: Positional encodings (PEs) enhance the power of graph neural networks (GNNs), both theoretically and empirically. Two of the most popular families of PEs - spectral (e.g., Laplacian eigenspaces, effective resistance) and walk-based (polynomials of the adjacency matrix) - are theoretically equivalent in expressive power, with expressivity between the 1-WL and 3-WL tests. However, this equivalence assumes the GNN uses the "complete" version of these PEs, which requires $O(n^3)$ time and space complexity. Instead, practitioners commonly use truncated variants of these encodings, such as the first $k$ eigenspaces or powers of the adjacency matrix. However, the theoretical properties of these truncated PEs are unknown. In this work, we initiate the study of these truncated PEs. Theoretically, we show that, under truncation, several families of PEs are fundamentally different in expressive power. As a corollary, we show that truncated spectral PEs are no longer stronger than the 1-WL test. We also study a family of spectral PEs, the $k$-harmonic distances, to highlight the differences in expressive power of even closely related truncated PEs. Finally, we experimentally show that a mix of truncated PEs is preferable to any single family on real-world datasets.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [266] [ToolSense: A Diagnostic Framework for Auditing Parametric Tool Knowledge in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12451)
*Ashutosh Hathidara,Sai Shruthi Sistla,Sebastian Schreiber,Sahil Bansal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ToolSense框架，用于诊断大语言模型在工具检索中的真实理解能力。研究发现，参数化工具检索模型在标准基准上表现优异，但在真实模糊查询和知识探测中性能大幅下降（最高下降50-64个百分点），揭示了"知识-检索分离"现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具检索基准（如ToolBench）使用冗长、充分指定的查询，且评估时采用约束解码限制输出，无法揭示模型是否真正理解工具。作者旨在构建一个诊断框架，系统性地评估参数化工具检索模型的真实工具理解能力。

Method: 提出ToolSense框架，包含三个自动生成的基准：(1) 现实检索基准（RRB），含三个模糊级别的查询；(2) MCQ探测基准；(3) QA探测基准。将该框架应用于ToolBench（约47k工具），评估五种参数化模型训练配置。

Result: 在RRB查询上，多种模型配置性能相比充分指定的ToolBench基准下降约50-64个百分点，甚至低于嵌入模型基线。此外，部分模型尽管检索表现强劲，但在事实探测上得分接近随机水平，呈现"知识-检索分离"现象。

Conclusion: 参数化工具检索模型存在知识-检索分离问题，表面优异的检索性能并不意味着模型真正理解工具语义。ToolSense框架和诊断基准已开源，可用于系统评估工具检索模型的真实能力。

Abstract: Large language models deployed as agents over large tool catalogs face a critical tool-retrieval bottleneck. As embedding-based retrieval approaches rely on compact encoders that may under-capture specialized tool semantics, parametric tool retrieval addresses this by encoding each tool as a virtual token appended to the LLM vocabulary, fine-tuned in two stages (memorization then retrieval SFT) to use the LLM as a retriever, achieving strong performance on standard ToolBench retrieval benchmarks. Yet these benchmarks use verbose, fully-specified queries, and their evaluation applies constrained decoding that restricts outputs to valid token paths, neither reveals whether the model actually understands its tools. We introduce \textbf{ToolSense}, an open-source LLM-powered diagnostic framework that takes any tool catalog as input and automatically generates three benchmarks: a Realistic Retrieval Benchmark (RRB) with queries at three ambiguity tiers, an MCQ probing benchmark, and a QA probing benchmark. Applying ToolSense to ToolBench (~47k tools) and evaluating five parametric model training configurations reveals a knowledge-retrieval dissociation: on RRB queries, several configurations collapse by ~50-64 percentage points compared to fully-specified ToolBench benchmarks, falling below the embedding-model baseline. Additionally, despite strong retrieval performance, some models score near-random on factual probes, suggesting a knowledge-retrieval dissociation. We open-source the ToolSense framework and the ToolBench diagnostic benchmarks at https://github.com/SAP/toolsense.

</details>


### [267] [Arbor: Tree Search as a Cognition Layer for Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12563)
*Neha Prakriya,Chaojun Hou,Zheng Gong,Huasha Zhao,Xi Zhao,Mou Li,Zhenyu Gu,Emad Barsoum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Arbor 是一个多智能体框架，将结构化树搜索作为认知层引入自主优化系统。通过在大型有状态动作空间中维护显式的评分假设搜索树作为共享工作记忆，Arbor 在全栈 LLM 推理优化上实现了最高 193% 的吞吐-延迟帕累托改进，远超单智能体方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自主优化系统在孤立目标上运行，采用无状态评估方式，难以应对大规模、有状态动作空间中的复杂优化问题。尤其在全栈 LLM 推理优化这种需要跨应用、框架、编译器、内核和硬件栈协同的领域，传统方法力不从心。

Method: Arbor 设计了三个核心机制：(1) 结构化树搜索作为多智能体共享的认知层，用评分假设树维护工作记忆，随每次测量演进；(2) Orchestrator（编排者）+ Critic（批评者）的双智能体制衡架构，前者驱动优化并委托领域专家，后者通过根因分析、内省和测量验证保障稳定性；(3) 将智能体能力分解为硬技能（领域专长）和软技能（协调协议），支持全自主的多日优化活动。

Result: 在 LLM 推理优化任务上，Arbor 相比供应商优化基线实现了最高 193% 的吞吐-延迟帕累托前沿改进；单智能体（无框架支撑）仅获得 +33% 吞吐提升，且数小时内不可恢复地崩溃。该方法跨多代硬件平台泛化，运行间方差在 2 个百分点以内，具备硬件无关性和可复现性。

Conclusion: Arbor 证明，将结构化树搜索引入多智能体系统的认知层，结合制衡式双智能体架构，能够在全栈系统优化这类复杂的、跨层次的工程任务上实现显著且可复现的性能突破，且具备良好的硬件泛化能力。

Abstract: Arbor is a multi-agent framework that introduces structured tree search as a cognition layer for autonomous agents operating in large, stateful action spaces. Prior autonomous optimization systems operate on isolated targets with stateless evaluation. Arbor instead maintains an explicit search tree of scored hypotheses that serves as the shared working memory across agents, evolving with every measurement, treating failures as diagnostic signal that reshapes subsequent exploration, and expanding as prior successes shift the bottleneck distribution.
  We validate Arbor on full-stack LLM inference optimization, a domain where achieving peak performance has historically required coordinated effort from engineering teams across the application, framework, compiler, kernel, and hardware stack. Arbor pairs an Orchestrator agent, which drives optimization by delegating to Domain Specialists across the inference stack, with a Critic agent that safeguards stability through root-cause analysis, introspection, and measurement validation -- a checks-and-balances architecture where neither agent can unilaterally drive the system. Agent capabilities are decomposed into hard skills (domain expertise) and soft skills (coordination protocols that determine how contributions compose), enabling fully autonomous multi-day campaigns. Arbor achieves up to 193% inference throughput-latency Pareto improvement over vendor-optimized baselines, while a single agent without the harness plateaus at +33% throughput improvement and crashes irrecoverably within hours. Arbor generalizes to multiple generations of hardware platform, and run-to-run variance is within 2 percentage points demonstrating that the method is hardware-agnostic and reproducible.

</details>


### [268] [Strategic Decision Support for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12587)
*Shayan Kiyani,Sima Noorani,George Pappas,Hamed Hassani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文重新审视经典决策支持框架，将其反转应用于以AI智能体为中心的场景。作者提出一个战略性决策支持框架，通过优化问题在控制"反事实错失支持误差"的前提下最小化支持调用次数，并开发了基于自适应阈值和随机探索的在线算法，无需分布假设即可可靠控制误差，同时显著减少支持使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代智能体系统中，AI代理代替人类成为核心决策者，而人类和工具退居辅助角色。这种角色反转引发可靠性担忧：智能体错误后果严重，且行为必须与人类目标和约束对齐。传统的决策支持理论（成本-价值权衡、不确定性量化）需要在这种新范式下重新审视。

Method: 作者将智能体决策支持建模为一个受约束的优化问题：在控制"反事实错失支持误差"（即智能体独自行动时，那些本可通过支持显著改善输出的实例概率）的前提下，最小化支持调用次数。在总体层面，最优策略被证明是支持价值分数的阈值规则。基于此，提出在线算法：自适应阈值调整结合随机探索，无需分布假设即可控制误差；同时引入在线校准方法进一步减少不必要的支持调用。框架可统一建模信息收集、人机协作和工具使用等多种场景。

Result: 实验覆盖信息收集、人机协作和工具使用等多种场景，结果表明该方法能够在实践中可靠地控制目标误差水平，同时大幅减少支持调用次数。

Conclusion: 该框架为以AI智能体为中心的战略性决策支持提供了理论基础和实用算法，统一了多种应用场景，在保证可靠性的前提下实现了高效的支持资源利用。

Abstract: Traditionally, decision support studies how humans use machine learning models to make better decisions. In modern agentic systems, this division of roles is increasingly reversed: AI agents act on behalf of users, while humans and tools becomes support mechanisms around them. This role reversal brings reliability concerns to the forefront, since agentic errors can be consequential and agent behavior must remain aligned with human goals and constraints. Departing from the classical view of decision support, we revisit its two basic principles, the cost--value tradeoff of seeking support and the role of uncertainty quantification, in a setting where AI agents are the central actors. We propose a framework for strategic decision support for AI agents through an optimization problem that minimizes support usage subject to controlling a counterfactual missed-support error: the probability that the agent acts alone on instances where support would have materially improved its output. At the population level, we show that the optimal policy is a threshold rule on the value of support. Building on this structure, we develop an online algorithm that adaptively thresholds such a score and uses randomized exploration to control missed-support error without distributional assumptions. We further introduce a calibration-on-the-fly method that reduces unnecessary support calls online. We instantiate this framework across diverse scenarios, including information gathering, human--AI collaboration, and tool use, showing how each can be modeled through the same strategic decision-support lens. Experiments across these settings show that our method reliably controls the target error while substantially reducing support usage in practice.

</details>


### [269] [Pythagoras-Prover: Advancing Efficient Formal Proving via Augmented Lean Formalisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12594)
*Joshua Ong Jun Leang,Zheng Zhao,Mihaela Cătălina Stoian,Qiyuan Xu,Haonan Li,Wenda Li,Shay B. Cohen,Eleonora Giunchiglia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Pythagoras-Prover 是一个面向实用计算预算的开源 Lean 定理证明器系列，包含自回归模型（4B/32B）和首个扩散式证明器（4B）。通过课程式 SFT、动态证明过滤和 ALF 数据增强，4B 模型以 ~1/167 参数超越 DeepSeek-Prover-V2-671B，32B 模型在 MiniF2F-Test 达到 93.0% 的新 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 Lean 定理证明器性能虽强，但训练和推理计算成本极高，根源在于稀缺的验证证明数据和形式证明搜索的长推理轨迹，导致 SFT 和采样代价昂贵。该工作旨在在实用计算预算下构建高效的开源证明器。

Method: 提出 Pythagoras-Prover 系列：(1) 构建按 easy/medium/hard 分层的 Lean 验证语料库，进行课程式 SFT，由易到难渐进训练；(2) 动态证明-推理过滤方案，在 8k token 上下文预算内保留信息丰富的证明轨迹；(3) 增强 Lean 形式化 (ALF)：对已知问题进行扰动生成变体，通过自蒸馏扩充训练信号，无需对每个变异实例进行形式验证；(4) 扩散式证明器概念验证，在推理时迭代精化 Lean 证明。

Result: Pythagoras-Prover-4B 在 MiniF2F-Test pass@32 上达 86.1%，超越 DeepSeek-Prover-V2-671B（82.4%），参数量少约 167 倍；Pythagoras-Prover-32B 在 MiniF2F-Test 上达 93.0% 的开源 SOTA，并在 PutnamBench 672 题中解决 93 题。在 ALF 生成的污染敏感基准 MiniF2F-ALF 上，32B 仍最强，4B 与 Goedel-Prover-V2-32B 持平。

Conclusion: 通过课程学习、动态过滤和数据增强等训练效率优化，小模型可在形式定理证明上超越大得多的模型；ALF 揭示了现有模型对题目表面形式的依赖；扩散式证明器作为概念验证展示了新的推理范式潜力。

Abstract: Modern Lean theorem provers achieve strong performance only with substantial training and inference compute, driven in part by scarce verified proof data and the long reasoning traces of formal proof search, making both supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and sampling expensive. We introduce Pythagoras-Prover, a compute-efficient open-source family of Lean theorem provers built for practical compute budgets. The family spans two generation paradigms: autoregressive models at 4B and 32B parameters, and a first proof-of-concept diffusion-based prover (4B) that iteratively refines Lean proofs at inference time. For training efficiency, we build a Lean-verified corpus stratified into easy, medium, and hard problems for curriculum SFT, so models acquire proof skills progressively from shorter, simpler proofs to longer, harder ones. During SFT, a dynamic proof-reasoning filtering scheme preserves informative proof traces while keeping each instance within an 8k-token context budget. We also introduce Augmented Lean Formalisation (ALF), which expands scarce verified corpora into variants of formal statements, populated via self-distillation for extra training signal without formally verifying every mutated instance. By perturbing known problems while preserving their formal character, ALF reduces reliance on any statement's surface form. Empirically, Pythagoras-Prover-4B surpasses DeepSeek-Prover-V2-671B at pass@32 on MiniF2F-Test (86.1% vs 82.4%) with ~167x fewer parameters, while Pythagoras-Prover-32B sets the open-source state of the art at 93.0% on MiniF2F-Test and solves 93 of 672 PutnamBench problems. We release MiniF2F-ALF, an ALF-mutated contamination-sensitive benchmark on which every evaluated model loses accuracy; here our 32B remains strongest and our 4B matches the prior state of the art, Goedel-Prover-V2-32B.

</details>


### [270] [PersonaDrive: Human-Style Retrieval-Augmented VLA Agents for Closed-Loop Driving Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12616)
*Mahmoud Srewa,Praneetsai Iddamsetty,Mohammad Abdullah Al Faruque,Salma Elmalaki*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PersonaDrive 是一个通过检索人类驾驶演示来调节视觉-语言-动作（VLA）驾驶智能体的流水线，使其产生激进、中立、保守等多种驾驶风格，无需按风格重新训练即可用于闭环仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有闭环驾驶仿真中的非自车交通代理通常行为单一，要么基于规则，要么训练为单一行为模式。近期工作虽引入风格变化，但依赖事后标签或LLM推断的奖励权重，缺乏直接从人类按特定风格指令驾驶的演示数据中学习的能力。

Method: PersonaDrive 包含三个阶段：(i) 使用图文联合相似度得分对每种风格的人类驾驶数据进行离线三元组挖掘；(ii) 训练一个轻量级检索头，将冻结的视觉特征与小型控制编码器在每种风格的数据库上融合；(iii) 微调单一的 VLA 骨干网络，将检索到的上下文点作为航点预测时的上下文行为演示。推理时仅需切换检索头查询的数据库即可切换风格。

Result: 在 Bench2Drive 上，PersonaDrive（无风格）驾驶得分比 SimLingo 高 4.6%，比 HiP-AD 高 2.5%；风格调节下，每种风格均取得最高驾驶得分（约 2% 范围内波动），其最弱风格也超过最强基线 DMW 5.4%；从保守到激进指令，平均速度和加速度分别提升 18% 和 25%。

Conclusion: PersonaDrive 证明了通过检索式上下文演示调节单一 VLA 骨干网络，可以有效实现风格多样化的非自车代理，且无需按风格重新训练，为闭环仿真中逼真的人类风格交通建模提供了新范式。

Abstract: Closed-loop driving simulators typically populate their environments with non-ego traffic agents that behave largely the same way, produced either by rule-based traffic managers or by learned models trained toward a single behavioral mode. Recent work introduces style variation through post-hoc labels on observational data or LLM-inferred reward weights, but these signals act as proxies for what a style should reward rather than demonstrations of humans explicitly asked to drive in that style. We introduce PersonaDrive, a pipeline that conditions a vision-language-action (VLA) driving agent on retrieved demonstrations from a style-instructed human driving dataset, in which participants drive CARLA leaderboard routes under aggressive, neutral, and conservative instructions on a driver-in-the-loop rig. The pipeline has three stages: (i) offline triplet mining over per-style human driving data using a combined image-text similarity score; (ii) training a lightweight retrieval head that fuses frozen visual features with a small control encoder over per-style databases; and (iii) fine-tuning a single VLA backbone to treat retrieved context points as in-context behavioral demonstrations during waypoint prediction. At inference, the same backbone is conditioned on any style by swapping which per-style database the retrieval head queries, so selecting a style requires no per-style retraining while enabling human-style, style-diverse non-ego agents for closed-loop simulation. On Bench2Drive, PersonaDrive (no style) improves the driving score by 4.6% over SimLingo and 2.5% over HiP-AD, and under style conditioning attains the highest driving score in every style within a roughly 2% band (its weakest style surpassing the strongest baseline, DMW, by 5.4%), while average speed and acceleration rise by 18% and 25% from the conservative to the aggressive instruction.

</details>


### [271] ["Did you lie?" Evaluating Lie Detectors across Model Scale and Belief-Verified Model Organisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12618)
*Alan Cooney,David Africa,Geoffrey Irving*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 现有谎言检测器在训练型模型生物上表现大幅下降，仅思维链判断保持较好性能，当前技术无法高置信度推断模型真实信念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估语言模型谎言检测器需要模型"真心相信与所言相反"的测试场景，但现有训练型模型生物常无法满足该要求，导致先前检测结果的解读存疑。

Method: 构建13个推理模型生物（通过思维链验证隐藏信念并可泛化至留出任务），以及Varied Deception测试集（涵盖多种诱导说谎动机的提示型说谎场景）；在此之上评估四种检测器：思维链判断、对数概率分类器、两种激活探针（含新提出的Did-You-Lie/DYL后续探针训练方法），覆盖31个2B到1T参数的开源模型。

Result: 提示型说谎场景下，四种检测器性能均随模型能力正缩放；但在训练型模型生物上，所有基于激活和对数概率的检测器性能急剧下降，DYL保留信号最多；仅思维链判断保持较强表现（平衡准确率0.82），部分原因在于验证过程偏好CoT可读信念。

Conclusion: 当前谎言检测器无法支持关于模型信念的高置信度断言，需探索新研究方向以克服现有限制；已开源数据集、模型生物及训练好的检测器。

Abstract: Robust lie detectors for language models could enable powerful techniques for auditing, monitoring, and post-hoc investigation of model behaviour, but evaluating them requires testbeds where models verifiably believe the opposite of what they say. We show that existing trained model organisms often fail this requirement, leaving prior positive and negative detection results difficult to interpret. We address this with 13 reasoning model organisms whose hidden beliefs are verified in chain-of-thought and shown to generalise to held-out tasks, alongside Varied Deception, a prompted-lying testbed covering a broad range of lie-inducing motivations. On these testbeds we evaluate four detectors: a chain-of-thought judge, a logprob classifier, and two activation probes, including Did-You-Lie (DYL), a new method for training follow-up probes. On prompted lying, across 31 open-weight models spanning 2B to 1T parameters, all four detectors show positive scaling with model capability. However, every activation- and logprob-based detector drops sharply on our trained model organisms, with DYL retaining the most signal; only the chain-of-thought judge remains strong, with 0.82 balanced accuracy, partly as an artefact of our verification process favouring CoT-readable beliefs. Current lie detectors therefore cannot support high-confidence claims about model beliefs, and we suggest research directions that may address some of their current limitations. We release our datasets, model organisms, and trained detectors.

</details>


### [272] [TrajGenAgent: A Hierarchical LLM Agent for Human Mobility Trajectory Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12657)
*Siyu Li,Toan Tran,Lingyi Zhao,Khurram Shafique,Li Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TrajGenAgent 是一个无需微调的语义感知分层LLM-Agent框架，通过两阶段编排器-工作者设计生成逼真的人类移动轨迹，并在行为与语义合理性上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模轨迹采集成本高且受隐私限制，现有LLM生成方法要么缺乏细粒度时空锚定，要么微调成本高且削弱通用推理能力，因此需要一种无需微调、兼顾统计精度与语义合理性的合成轨迹生成方法。

Method: 提出TrajGenAgent，采用两阶段编排器-工作者架构：(1) LLM通过上下文学习从历史证据中合成个体和星期条件化的活动链；(2) 确定性工作流将每个活动具象化为完整访问记录，包括个性化POI检索、距离感知位置选择、运动学感知的行程时间传播及基于LLM的停留时长估计。同时引入基于异常检测的双检测器评估框架，从行为和语义两个维度衡量轨迹真实性。

Result: 在基准数据集和大规模仿真数据集上，TrajGenAgent在时空保真度、语义一致性和个体特定行为真实性方面均优于代表性的神经网络基线和LLM基线方法。

Conclusion: TrajGenAgent证明了通过分层LLM-Agent设计，可在不进行模型微调的情况下生成高质量、语义合理且行为逼真的合成人类移动轨迹，为交通规划、疫情控制等应用提供了低成本且隐私友好的数据生成方案。

Abstract: Human mobility data is important for transportation, urban planning, and epidemic control, but large-scale trajectory collection is often costly and privacy-constrained, motivating realistic synthetic trajectory generation. Existing LLM-based generators typically rely on either prompt engineering, which preserves zero-shot reasoning but lacks fine-grained spatiotemporal grounding, or trajectory-level fine-tuning, which improves statistical precision but incurs substantial computational cost and may weaken general reasoning. We propose TrajGenAgent, a semantic-aware hierarchical LLM-agent framework for human mobility trajectory generation without model fine-tuning. TrajGenAgent uses a two-stage orchestrator-worker design: an LLM first synthesizes an individual- and weekday-conditioned activity chain from historical evidence via in-context learning, and a deterministic workflow then grounds each activity into a complete visit using personalized POI retrieval, distance-aware location selection, kinematics-aware travel-time propagation, and LLM-based duration estimation. To evaluate realism beyond aggregate spatiotemporal statistics, we introduce an anomaly-detection-based evaluation framework using two complementary detectors to assess behavioral and semantic plausibility. Experiments on benchmark and large-scale simulation datasets show that TrajGenAgent improves spatiotemporal fidelity, semantic coherence, and individual-specific behavioral realism over representative neural and LLM-based baselines, while avoiding parameter updates.

</details>


### [273] [Evoflux: Inference-Time Evolution of Executable Tool Workflows for Compact Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12674)
*Kushal Raj Bhandari,Ling Yue,Ching-Yun Ko,Dhaval Patel,Shaowu Pan,Pin-Yu Chen,Jianxi Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Evoflux是一种推理时进化搜索方法，通过结构化编辑、执行反馈和多样性剪枝来修复小型语言模型生成的可执行工具工作流，在MCP-Bench上将执行可行性从约3%提升至17-24%，优于SFT、DPO和ReAct等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型语言模型在MCP式工具使用中面临核心挑战：需要从动态工具目录中发现工具、满足schema约束、保持中间输出间的依赖关系，并将最终响应建立在执行证据之上。小模型常生成看似合理但在工具解析、参数验证、依赖追踪或实际执行时失败的工作流图。关键问题在于，少量教师轨迹的蒸馏只能教授工作流格式，无法覆盖在不断变化的工具目录下修复失败计划所需的恢复行为。

Method: 提出Evoflux，一种推理时进化搜索方法，将紧凑工具使用建模为可执行工具工作流的修复问题。该方法通过以下机制进化带类型的工作流图：(1)结构化编辑；(2)执行反馈驱动评估；(3)自适应变异强度；(4)元引导重设计；(5)多样性剪枝。整个过程在推理时进行，不依赖大规模训练数据。

Result: 在包含实时MCP服务器和250个工具的MCP-Bench留出任务上，Evoflux将小型规划器的执行可行性从约3%提升至17-24%。相比之下，基于相同搜索挖掘数据进行SFT和SFT+DPO的方法表现持平和零样本性能相近或更差；ReAct虽能达到更高峰值，但方差更大且token成本更高。

Conclusion: 执行驱动的搜索在教师轨迹数据稀缺的条件下比蒸馏方法更可靠。Evoflux证明了推理时进化搜索可以有效补偿小型语言模型在工具使用中的规划缺陷，为资源受限场景下的智能体部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Compact language models (LMs) reduce cost, latency, and deployment risk for tool agents. Yet MCP-style tool use requires more than isolated function calling: an agent must discover tools from live catalogs, satisfy schemas, preserve dependencies across intermediate outputs, and ground final responses in executed evidence. Small planners often generate plausible workflow graphs that fail under tool resolution, parameter validation, dependency tracking, or execution. We argue that this failure mode is poorly handled by small-corpus distillation. A few hundred teacher traces can teach workflow format, but rarely cover the recovery behavior needed to repair failed plans over changing tool catalogs. We introduce Evoflux, an inference-time evolutionary search method that treats compact tool use as the repair of executable tool workflows. It evolves typed workflow graphs through structured edits, execution feedback, adaptive intensity, meta-guided redesign, and diversity pruning. On held-out MCP-Bench tasks spanning live MCP servers and 250 tools, Evoflux raises execution feasibility from roughly 3% to 17-24% across small planners. In contrast, SFT and SFT+DPO on the same search-mined data match, underperform, or collapse below zero-shot performance; ReAct reaches higher peaks, but with higher variance and token cost. These results show that execution-grounded search is more reliable under scarce teacher-trace budgets.

</details>


### [274] [From AGI to ASI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12683)
*Tim Genewein,Matija Franklin,Alexander Lerchner,Laurent Orseau,Samuel Albanie,Adam Bales,Cole Wyeth,Stephanie Chan,Iason Gabriel,Joel Z. Leibo,Allan Dafoe,Marcus Hutter,Thore Graepel,Shane Legg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本报告探讨了从人类水平AGI向人工超级智能（ASI）过渡的路径，提出了四条潜在途径（规模扩展、范式转变、递归改进、多智能体涌现），并分析了摩擦与瓶颈，强调AI进展可能持续加速，社会需做好跨学科全球性准备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去十年，AGI从远景猜想变为大型AI组织的具体目标。AGI的实现将对人类社会产生深远影响，但关于后AGI时代机器智能如何沿着智能连续体继续发展的问题尚未得到充分探讨，特别是从AGI到ASI的过渡阶段亟需系统性分析。

Method: 以通用AI（Universal AI）的理论框架为形式化基础，采用概念分析与路径推演方法。首先对ASI进行特征刻画，然后识别并阐述四条从AGI到ASI的潜在发展路径，最后分析各路径中可能遇到的摩擦与瓶颈，并据此提出开放性研究问题。

Result: 识别出四条ASI发展路径：(1) AGI规模扩展；(2) AI范式转变；(3) 递归自我改进；(4) 大规模多智能体集体涌现ASI。分析表明，这些路径上的摩擦可能从可忽略到实质性阻碍不等，存在巨大不确定性，无法排除AI进展在未来几年持续加速的可能性。这意味着AGI可能并非单一突变事件，而更可能是一系列由AI驱动的科技突破引发的连续社会变革。

Conclusion: AGI到ASI的过渡充满不确定性，传统的"单步突变"图景可能不准确，更恰当的是"连续变革"视角。为此，需要全球范围内的跨学科协作来应对这一前景，并解决所提出的开放性研究问题。

Abstract: Over the last decade, building human-level artificial general intelligence has moved from far-fetched speculation to being a concrete next-decade target for many of the largest AI organisations. Achieving this goal would have profound and far-reaching impacts on human society, which raises many complex questions for the decade ahead. This report investigates how AI itself might continue to develop in a post-AGI world along the continuum of machine intelligence. The endpoint of this continuum, Universal AI, is theoretically well understood, which provides some formal grounding for the main focus of this report: the transition from human-level AGI to artificial general superintelligence, which, intuitively, can be understood as a system that is more intelligent and cognitively capable than large organisations of humans. After characterizing ASI, the report discusses four potential pathways from AGI to ASI: scaling AGI, AI paradigm shifts, recursive improvement, and ASI emerging from large-scale multi-agent collectives. The report then discusses possible frictions and bottlenecks along these pathways. Determining whether the impact of these frictions will be negligible or substantial raises a number of concrete open research questions. Due to large uncertainties for predicting ASI progress, it cannot be ruled out that AI progress might continue to accelerate over the next years. This could imply that the image of a single transformative step change, caused by the introduction of human-level AGI into our society, could be inaccurate. More apt might be the prospect of a series of transformative societal changes caused by AI-enabled progress and breakthroughs across many areas of science and technology. Preparing for this prospect requires a massively interdisciplinary endeavour of global scope and interest.

</details>


### [275] [Deployment-Centered Evaluation: Predicting Query-Level Rejection Risk in a Clinical LLM System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12702)
*Alyssa Unell,Miguel Fuentes,Brenna Li,Bridget Lin,Meena Jagadeesan,Sanmi Koyejo,Nigam Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对嵌入电子健康记录系统的LLM进行部署中心化评估，利用部署特有上下文训练预响应分类器预测用户拒绝风险（AUROC 0.719），并证明部署上下文比仅用查询内容更能提升预测能力，为定向护栏机制打开了可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM临床评估依赖静态基准测试，只衡量正确性而忽略用户接受度、聚合查询掩盖个体差异、且需要密集标注数据，造成临床部署评估存在重大盲区。需要一种反映真实部署条件、利用稀疏用户反馈的评估方法。

Method: 在学术医疗中心的电子健康记录系统中部署LLM，收集4.5个月的用户反馈（接受/拒绝）。训练一个预响应分类器，在生成回复前仅基于查询内容和部署特有上下文（提供商类型、科室名称、所用语言模型）预测用户拒绝风险。下游评估两个应用场景：护栏触发和弃权。

Result: 预测模型达到AUROC 0.719。核心发现：引入部署特有上下文（而非仅使用查询内容）显著提升了预测用户拒绝的能力。验证了利用稀疏真实反馈进行拒绝预测的可行性。

Conclusion: 利用部署特有上下文预测用户对LLM输出的拒绝是可行的，这为在临床系统中部署定向护栏机制提供了实证基础，推动了从"正确性评估"向"用户接受度评估"的范式转变。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into clinical systems, making it essential to evaluate the real-world utility of these systems. However, static benchmarks tend to measure correctness rather than user acceptance, aggregate performance across queries, and require densely annotated datasets -- leading to major blind spots for evaluating clinical systems. In this work, we perform a deployment-centered evaluation of an LLM system embedded within electronic health records at an academic medical center, where user feedback is sparse but closely reflects the deployment conditions. Specifically, we train a pre-response classifier that estimates the risk that a future interaction will result in the user rejecting the LLM response, based on query content and deployment-specific context available before generation. We conduct a prospective analysis of our model over 4.5 months of user feedback, finding that our prediction model achieves an AUROC of 0.719. Further, we estimate the benefit of such predictions in two downstream use cases (guardrail triggering and abstention). Our key conceptual insight is that making use of deployment-specific context (i.e., the provider type, department name, language model used for response), as opposed to only query content, improves the ability to predict whether the user will reject the system output. Altogether, our empirical case study demonstrates the feasibility of predicting user rejection using deployment-specific context, opening the door to targeted guardrails.

</details>


### [276] [Definitional alignment before capability alignment: a Design-Science framework for adjudicating claims about AGI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12713)
*J. E. Aguilera Briones*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对"AGI是否已到来"的争议源于定义不统一这一核心问题，提出DAF-AGI这一二阶概念工件，包含五条序数评估标准和一套结构化治理审计框架，并在多种定义家族和具体主张上进行了演示与压力测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AGI缺乏单一、共享且稳定的指称对象，不同的操作性定义可能对同一系统给出截然相反的判断。这种"欠明确化"不仅造成学术争论，更是一个亟待解决的设计与治理问题。

Method: 遵循设计科学研究方法论（DSRM），开发了DAF-AGI——一个二阶概念工件。其包含两个耦合组件：（1）五条序数标准，用于评估候选AGI定义的"裁决适格性"；（2）一套结构化治理审计，涵盖作者身份、利益关联、认证机制、外部验证和修订权限。该工件在五种主流测量家族及一种紧缩性边界立场上进行了演示，并针对"当前生成式系统因在许多认知任务上超越受过良好教育的成人而构成AGI"这一强主张进行了压力测试。

Result: 基于2024-2025年引用的证据，该强主张仅在基于性能的操作化定义下可被认证；能力本体论、心理测量学和技能习得方法均未认证该主张，经济学家族仍不确定，紧缩性立场则拒绝二元裁决。贡献在于新颖的整合与操作化，而非实证验证。

Conclusion: 本文进一步提出"定义主权"作为"算法主权"的使能组件：即在公共问责下对引入的技术类别进行争议、认证和修订的制度性能力。独立应用、评分者间信度测试及作者外部案例验证仍有待开展。

Abstract: Claims that artificial general intelligence has already arrived and claims that it remains decades away are often defended from overlapping evidence. "AGI" lacks a single shared and stable referent and competing operationalizations can return different verdicts on the same system. This article treats that under-specification as a design and governance problem. Following Design Science Research Methodology, it develops DAF-AGI, a second-order conceptual artifact with two coupled components: five ordinal criteria for assessing the adjudicative fitness of candidate definitions and a structured governance audit of authorship, interest, certification, external verification and revision authority. The artifact is demonstrated on five prominent measurement families and one deflationary boundary position in a documented corpus and then stress-tested against a stylized strong arrival claim: that current generative systems constitute AGI because they outperform a well-educated adult on many cognitive tasks. On evidence from the cited 2024-2025 sources, the claim was certifiable only under a performance-based operationalization; capability-ontology, psychometric and skill-acquisition approaches did not certify it, the economic family remains indeterminate and the deflationary position refuses binary adjudication. The contribution is a novel integration and operationalization, not an empirical validation: independent application, inter-rater testing and author-external cases remain necessary. The paper further proposes definitional sovereignty as an enabling component of algorithmic sovereignty: the institutional capacity to contest, certify and revise imported technological categories under public accountability.

</details>


### [277] [The Theory of Mind Utility: Formal Specification of a Mentalizing Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12721)
*Nikolos Gurney,Stacy Marsella*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ToM-U 在计算层面形式化了心智理论中的认知状态推断问题，通过构建局部认知世界模型（LEWM）并对候选模型进行离散评估，在不预设信念状态的前提下，为读心机制提供了可证伪的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有心智理论的形式化框架（如贝叶斯ToM）预设而非推导信念状态，而模拟论和理论论又缺乏认知状态推断的形式化工具；需要一个计算层面的形式化框架来明确读心（mentalizing）到底计算什么、为何如此计算。

Method: 提出ToM-U，核心是构建局部认知世界模型（LEWM）——一种有向类型图，包含智能体节点、状态节点及其认知关系。包含五项形式化定义：(1) LEWM结构；(2) 智能体节点属性，包括有序信息访问历史；(3) 递归读心的有界增殖机制；(4) 三种推断程序；(5) 残差函数，捕获读心失败遗留下的结构化痕迹。通过对候选LEWM进行离散评估直至达到充分置信度来完成推断。

Result: ToM-U 区别于贝叶斯ToM等邻近框架，因为它推导而非预设信念状态；也区别于模拟论和理论论，因为它提供了认知状态推断的形式化工具。该架构能从模型的结构属性（而非辅助假设）中产生关于读心失败的方向性、可证伪预测，并将自身定位为上游于目标推断及其他社会认知过程的、领域无关的通用机制。

Conclusion: ToM-U 为认知状态推断提供了计算层面的严格形式化，填补了现有理论的形式化空白，其结构驱动的预测能力使其成为可检验的社会认知计算理论框架。

Abstract: Inferring others' beliefs requires more than reading surface signals; it requires tracking who told them what, in what order, and how credibly. The Theory of Mind Utility (ToM-U) formalizes this epistemic state inference problem at the computational level of analysis, specifying what mentalizing computes and why without commitment to algorithmic or neural implementation. ToM-U achieves this by constructing Local Epistemic World Models (LEWMs) -- directed typed graphs that represent agents, state nodes, and the epistemic relationships among them -- and evaluating discrete candidate LEWMs against observed behavior until one achieves sufficient confidence. Five formal definitions specify the LEWM structure, agent node properties including ordered information access history, a bounded proliferation mechanism for recursive mentalizing, three inference procedures, and a residue function that captures the structured trace left by failed mentalizing attempts. ToM-U differs from Bayesian Theory of Mind and adjacent formal accounts, which presuppose rather than derive belief states, and from simulation theory and theory-theory, which lack a formal apparatus for epistemic state inference. The architecture generates directional, falsifiable predictions about mentalizing failure that follow from structural properties of the model rather than auxiliary assumptions, and positions ToM-U as a domain-agnostic mechanism upstream of goal inference and other downstream social cognitive processes.

</details>


### [278] [Rethinking Psychometric Evaluation of LLMs: When and Why Self-Reports Predict Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12730)
*Rafal Kocielnik,Pengrui Han,Peiyang Song,Myrl G. Marmarelis,Ramit Debnath,Dean Mobbs,Anima Anandkumar,R. Michael Alvarez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对比了大五人格与计划行为理论(TPB)在预测LLM行为一致性上的效果，发现TPB在共享对话中能达到人类水平的SR-行为一致性，但在跨对话场景中仅对训练塑造的隐性偏见等行为保持连贯，而对阿谀奉承等受提示强 priming 的行为则失效；此外，角色提示使自我报告更一致，但并未使行为对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究发现LLM存在自我报告与行为之间的脱节，但该结论基于大五人格这一对特定行为预测力本身就很弱的框架，且未控制会话隔离与上下文匹配等条件。作者希望检验：使用更具行为针对性的TPB框架、并系统操控会话上下文后，LLM是否真正缺乏SR-行为一致性。

Method: 在4个行为任务和11个前沿LLM上运行实验，对比大五人格与计划行为理论(TPB)两种框架；系统操控会话上下文（共享对话 vs 独立对话）和身份诱导（persona prompting），测量自我报告与实际行为之间的一致性（coherence）。

Result: 1）共享对话中，TPB达到人类水平的SR-行为一致性，大五人格则未达到；2）跨独立对话时，一致性仅在训练塑造的隐性偏见等不依赖即时提示的行为上存活，而在被上下文强priming的行为（如阿谀奉承）上崩溃；3）角色提示使自我报告跨对话更一致，但并未使行为与报告对齐。

Conclusion: 粗糙的人格框架（如大五）不适合用于测试LLM部署行为；需要更特定于任务和行为的测量工具，且这些工具也必须在不同任务和上下文中评估其有效性。

Abstract: Anticipating LLM behavioral tendencies from low-cost psychometric probes is critical for safe deployment, but only if self-reports (SR) reliably predict behavior. Recent work documented substantial SR-behavior dissociation in LLMs, but relied on broad personality traits (Big 5) that predict specific behaviors weakly, even in humans. Furthermore, the isolation of conversational sessions combined with weak context matching left open whether LLMs truly lack coherence or whether the conditions needed to detect such coherence were not met. We contrast Big 5 with the Theory of Planned Behavior (TPB), which measures intention targeted to a specific behavior and predicts human behavior substantially better than broad traits. We run experiments across four behavioral tasks and 11 frontier LLMs, while also varying session context and identity induction. We find that SR-behavior coherence exists but is selective. 1) Within a shared conversation, the Theory of Planned Behavior reaches human-level coherence; Big 5 does not. 2) Across separate conversations, coherence survives only for behaviors anchored outside the immediate prompt, such as implicit bias shaped by training, and collapses when behavior is strongly primed by context, as with sycophancy. 3) Persona prompting makes self-reports more consistent across conversations, but does not bring behavior into alignment. These findings suggest that coarse personality frameworks, such as Big 5 may not be the best tools for testing deployment behavior. More task- and behavior-specific instruments are needed, and even these must be evaluated across tasks and contexts.

</details>


### [279] [Benchmarking AI Agents for Addressing Scientific Challenges Across Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12736)
*Tianyu Liu,Allen Xin Wang,Antonia Panescu,Lisa Xinyi Chen,Wenxin Long,Xinyu Wei,Yueqian Jing,Ziyao Zeng,Jihang Chen,Sihan Jiang,Ziqing Wang,Siyi Gu,Siyu Chen,Xinyang Hu,Haoran Shao,Leqi Xu,Wangjie Zheng,Zhiyuan Cao,Ada Fang,Botao Yu,Kunyang Sun,Rex Ying,Arman Cohan,Qingyu Chen,Lingzhou Xue,Kaize Ding,Yuanqi Du,Wengong Jin,Zhuoran Yang,Marinka Zitnik,James Zou,Hua Xu,Hongyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SciAgentArena 是一个包含约200个任务的系统性基准测试，用于在真实科研场景中评估 AI agent。当前 agent 在结构化数据分析流程中表现良好，但在生成新颖见解、自主探索和解决开放式研究问题方面仍然挣扎。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI agent 基准测试无法捕捉科研工作的复杂性、异质性和长程推理需求；而科学任务基准又将研究简化为静态问题，缺乏交互式评估支持。两者之间存在明显空白。

Method: 构建 SciAgentArena 基准，包含约200个来自多领域真实科研需求的任务，配备逐步验证机制和与 agent 无关的交互式评估环境。

Result: 当前 agent 在任务结构和评估标准明确的数据分析流程中贡献显著，但跨科学场景表现不均：难以生成真正新颖的见解、维持自主探索、为开放式问题制定稳健方案。论文进一步归纳了常见失败模式。

Conclusion: SciAgentArena 为衡量科学 AI agent 进展提供了实用框架，可指导未来能够应对复杂科学挑战的 agent 设计。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly being developed to accelerate scientific discovery, yet their practical capabilities in real research settings remain poorly understood. Existing benchmarks for AI agents rarely capture the complexity, heterogeneity, and extended reasoning required by scientific work, whereas benchmarks for scientific tasks often reduce research to static, direct problems and provide limited support for interactive evaluation. Here, we introduce SciAgentArena, a systematic benchmark for evaluating AI agents in real-world scientific research scenarios drawn from emerging needs across multiple domains. SciAgentArena comprises approximately 200 tasks with stepwise verification and an interactive, agent-agnostic environment for assessing diverse AI agents. Using this benchmark, we find that current agents can contribute effectively to well-specified data-analysis workflows, particularly when the task structure and evaluation criteria are clear. However, their performance remains uneven across scientific contexts: agents struggle to generate genuinely novel insights, sustain self-directed exploration, and formulate robust solutions for open-ended research questions. We further characterize common failure modes across agents and identify opportunities for improving their reliability, autonomy, and scientific reasoning. Together, SciAgentArena provides a practical framework for measuring progress in AI agents for science and for guiding the design of future agents capable of addressing complex scientific challenges. Full codes, tasks, and datasets can be accessed via this link: https://sciagentarena.github.io/.

</details>


### [280] [Reducing the Complexity of Deep Learning Models for EEG Analysis on Wearable Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12742)
*Farough Shayeste Roodi,Parham Zilouchian Moghaddam,Mahdi Mohammadi-nasab,Mehdi Modarressi,Mostafa Ersali Salehi Nasab,Masoud Daneshtalab*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在资源受限的可穿戴设备上部署用于EEG信号分析（癫痫检测）的深度神经网络模型的可行性，重点研究参数量化和电极削减方法在准确率与计算复杂度之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
